# Exact Profile Qualification Increments

All 38 profiles have distinct open checklists below. First qualification targets are i8080a for a small little-endian path and mc6800 for an early big-endian cross-check; these are scheduling choices, not changes to specification compatibility. Subsequent family/model order follows dependency readiness. No profile passes on a sibling receipt or by schema validation alone.

Source: [specification index](../specs/README.md). Delivery and evidence rules: [00](00_delivery_and_evidence.md). All checkboxes are initially open. Commands and implementation paths below are planned interfaces, not existing tools. Each listed row is one increment with a 15-minute active-work ceiling.

## N01: mos6502

Target `smil.ref.mos6502.checked.v2`; family 6502; register/address bits 8 / 16; little, cap 1; incoming packet A:low8, X:high8. Paths: test/native/profiles/mos6502/ and examples/smil/. Each conformance row consumes existing implementations; any newly discovered implementation fix is a new child increment, not hidden work in the test row.

| Increment / estimate | Prerequisites | Bounded deliverable | Demonstration and pass condition | Requirements advanced |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="n01-01"></a>- [ ] **N01-01** / 10 min | CP01, T009 | mos6502: Register only this exact adopted manifest/components/digests and single-agent domain. | Validator rejects changed component/manifest/shared digest, unknown field, unsupported width and wrong board permission; data/ROM mappings and layout formulas checked. | REF-001, REF-002, REF-003, REF-005, REF-006, REF-007, REF-012, REF-016 |
| <a id="n01-02"></a>- [ ] **N01-02** / 10 min | N01-01, K01-34 | mos6502: Bind this exact CPU/mode to an independently verified engine/legality adapter; do not silently select nearby CPU. | Known legal/illegal/mode-specific fixture runs with pinned engine+ISA provenance. Unsupported engine mode leaves this profile blocked, even when family fixtures pass. | REF-004, RCPU-001, RCPU-002, RCPU-003 |
| <a id="n01-03"></a>- [ ] **N01-03** / 10 min | N01-02 | mos6502: Wire only this model native-address/stack/alignment/monitor/clobber traits to the family emitter. | Hand-byte/native-state boundary sample validates physical translation, packet register and preserved resources; sibling-mode sample must fail or differ as contracted. | REF-017, PKT-007, FRM-003, RCPU-003 |
| <a id="n01-04"></a>- [ ] **N01-04** / 10 min | N01-03, R022, Z009, L021 | mos6502: Compile/link/load/run one constant-return smil_main through real bootstrap and runtime object. | Independent emulator executes emitted native bytes and reports exact i32 status33 via offsets2..6, with no host interpreter shortcut; save image, map and native trace. | RRT-004, ENV-001, ENV-002, RIMG-001, RIMG-002, LNK-010 |
| <a id="n01-05"></a>- [ ] **N01-05** / 10 min | N01-04 | mos6502: Run bounded arithmetic/flags/shift/divide shard at widths 1,3,12 including native-width mismatches. | All independent expected values/faults match; srem MIN/-1 returns0, oversized counts unmasked, negative division/shift rounding distinct; compiler and emulator exit evidence saved. | INT-001, INT-002, INT-003, INT-004, INT-005, INT-006, INT-007, INT-008, INT-009, INT-010, INT-011, INT-012, INT-013, INT-014, INT-015, INT-016, INT-017, INT-018, INT-019, INT-020, INT-021, SMIL-003, SMIL-009 |
| <a id="n01-06"></a>- [ ] **N01-06** / 10 min | N01-04 | mos6502: Run bounded width24/64 helper shard plus max-width support/placement boundary cases. | Non-native i64 divide/carry works when image fits; huge statically impossible type/frame reports placement diagnostic, never narrower values. Seeded shards stay under 120s. | INT-001, INT-002, INT-003, INT-004, INT-005, INT-006, INT-007, INT-008, INT-009, INT-010, INT-011, INT-012, INT-013, INT-014, INT-015, INT-016, INT-017, INT-018, INT-019, INT-020, INT-021, REF-005, RRT-003 |
